"""Read actual Pi/Critic encoder inputs without mutating models or physics."""
import torch
from matlab144_goal_conditioning_v1 import configure_leg_goal_conditioning
from matlab144_tracking_readback_v2 import check as tracking_check


def check(mdp,pi,critic,state,frames):
    metadata=mdp.provider.metadata
    configure_leg_goal_conditioning(mdp.provider.names,metadata['joint_position_midpoint30'],metadata['joint_position_range_scale30'])
    modules={'pi':pi,'critic':critic}
    before={n:{k:v.detach().clone() for k,v in m.named_buffers()} for n,m in modules.items()}
    captured={}
    def capture(name):
        def hook(module,args):captured[name]=args[0].detach().clone()
        return hook
    hp=pi.enc.register_forward_pre_hook(capture('pi'))
    hc=critic.net.register_forward_pre_hook(capture('critic'))
    try:
        with torch.no_grad():
            a=state[0].new_zeros(*frames.shape,6)
            xi=state[0].new_zeros(*frames.shape,31)
            obs=mdp.obs(state,a,xi,frames)
            goal=mdp.goal(frames,10)
            normalized_pi=pi.goal_norm(goal)
            output=pi(obs[...,None,:].expand(*frames.shape,10,86),goal)
            features=mdp.critic_features(state,frames,mdp.normalize_context(xi))
            critic_input=torch.cat((features,goal),-1)
            normalized_critic=critic.norm(critic_input)[...,-1460:]
            value=critic(critic_input)
            assert torch.isfinite(output).all() and torch.isfinite(value).all()
            literal=all(torch.equal(v,before[n][k]) for n,m in modules.items() for k,v in m.named_buffers())
            assert literal,'Readback changed a normalization buffer'
            result=tracking_check(mdp,pi,critic,state,frames=frames,
                normalized_pi_goal=normalized_pi,normalized_critic_goal=normalized_critic,
                actual_pi_conditioned_goal=captured['pi'],actual_critic_conditioned_goal=captured['critic'][...,-1460:],
                norm_literal_receipt={'passed':literal,'all_supplied_Pi_Critic_named_buffers_equal':True})
            assert result['passed'],'Actual fixed-unit Pi/Critic encoder readback failed'
            result.update(actual_encoder_forward_hooks_used=True,actual_Pi_output_abs_max=float(output.abs().max()),
                actual_Critic_value_abs_max=float(value.abs().max()),no_optimizer_or_physics=True,
                source_or_precontrol_pose_with_typed_reference_phase_goals=True,
                reference_is_goal_not_WM_transition_truth=True)
            return result
    finally:
        hp.remove();hc.remove()

"""Existing authorized stdin-memory transport; no secrets written or echoed."""
from pathlib import Path
import importlib.util,json,sys,threading,time
N=Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent;T=N.parent;W=T.parent
def fresh_cim(path):
    raw=Path(path).read_bytes();v=json.loads(raw)
    assert 0<=time.time()-v['epoch']<=30 and v['exact_known_control_finite_matches']==[],'Fresh known finite-client inventory required before marker/network'
    return v
def module(name,path):
    sp=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(name,path);m=importlib.util.module_from_spec(sp);sys.modules[name]=m;sp.loader.exec_module(m);return m
class Session:
    def __init__(self,label):self.label=label;self.client=None;self.secret=None;self.transport=None;self.previous=None;self.stop=threading.Event()
    def __enter__(self):
        wrapper=module('serial165_wrapper_'+self.label,T/'H1_STEP270_REWARD_PATH_V1_20261022/same_authorized_session_compressed_transport_v227.py')
        self.transport,self.previous=wrapper.install()
        sys.path.insert(0,str(W/'MOD4_TWO_LINUX_POLICY50_COHORT_52_20261021'))
        import deploy_two50_reconciled_original_writer_once as d
        self.d=d
        sec=module('serial165_secret_'+self.label,W/'MOD4_BUDGET_ADD100H_EIGHTH_20261021/run_authorized_cohort50_with_existing_hidden_stdin_memory.py')
        def progress():
            while not self.stop.wait(25):print(json.dumps(dict(stage='SAME_HANDLE_ONE165_'+self.label,epoch=time.time())),flush=True)
        self.thread=threading.Thread(target=progress,daemon=True);self.thread.start()
        try:self.secret=sec.recover();self.client=d.connect72(self.secret);self.secret=None;return self
        except BaseException:self.__exit__(None,None,None);raise
    def code(self,source,value,timeout):return self.d.code(self.client,self.d.PY72,source,value,timeout=timeout)
    def __exit__(self,*args):
        self.secret=None;self.stop.set()
        if hasattr(self,'thread'):self.thread.join(1)
        if self.client:self.client.close()
        if self.transport:self.transport.SESSION=self.previous
        return False
